2026-10-24T00:29:17.5893533Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5894962Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5896097Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5897865Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5898679Z #
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5899116Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5899788Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5900814Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5901744Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5902672Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5903472Z #
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5903903Z set -eu
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5904757Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5905049Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5905556Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5906052Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5906567Z do
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5907006Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5907494Z         in
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5907965Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5908521Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5909183Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5909855Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5910314Z     esac
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5910740Z done
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5911047Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5911437Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5911995Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5912281Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5912829Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5913355Z then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5913822Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5914615Z fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5914982Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5915444Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5916237Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-24T00:29:17.5917023Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5917688Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5918377Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5919254Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5919940Z     #
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5920637Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5921511Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5922229Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5923252Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5923921Z     else
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5925352Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5926341Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5926823Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5927114Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5928031Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5934769Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5935584Z       fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5936053Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5936375Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5936722Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5937520Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5938188Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5938589Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5939364Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5940178Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5941102Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5941764Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5942403Z     else
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5942964Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5943602Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5943958Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5944662Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5945379Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5946033Z }

2026-10-24T00:29:17.5946712Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5947680Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5948942Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5949807Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5950347Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5950706Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5951070Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5951812Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5952638Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5953467Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5953967Z         fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5954738Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5955253Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5955813Z     done
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5956141Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5956754Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5957962Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5958876Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5959691Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5960886Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5962576Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5963136Z     else
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5963772Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5964844Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5965340Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5965777Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5966076Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5966367Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {

2026-10-24T00:29:17.5967263Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5967776Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5968722Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5969588Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5970223Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5970596Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5971544Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5972125Z     then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5972940Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5973749Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5974665Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5975311Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5975944Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5976378Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5977239Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5978009Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5978523Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5979031Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5979733Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5980039Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5980945Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5981772Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5982487Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5983434Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5983732Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5984670Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5985006Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5985191Z

2026-10-24T00:29:17.5986068Z then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5986542Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5987086Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5987594Z else

2026-10-24T00:29:17.5995425Z

2026-10-24T00:29:17.5996453Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5998630Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-24T00:29:17.5999449Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6000137Z             sleep 0.5;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6003193Z         done
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6003761Z     else

2026-10-24T00:29:17.6005881Z         sleep 3
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6006547Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6006963Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6007919Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6009129Z

2026-10-24T00:29:17.6013732Z     else
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6014551Z         BROWSER=(${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD})

2026-10-24T00:29:17.6016458Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6017029Z     # start browser in a temporary home dir, so that it does not interfere with your real one
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6018154Z     BROWSER_HOME=$(mktemp --directory --tmpdir cockpit.desktop.XXXXXX)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6019066Z     HOME="$BROWSER_HOME" XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=GNOME \
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6020352Z     pkexec --user $INSTALLER_USER env DISPLAY=$DISPLAY "${user_environment[@]}" "${BROWSER[@]}" http://"$WEBUI_ADDRESS""$URL_PATH" &

2026-10-24T00:29:17.6029548Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6030090Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6030383Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6031515Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6032144Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6032961Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6033556Z fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6033914Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6035304Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6036274Z strstr() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6036789Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6037365Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6037725Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6038027Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6038646Z action() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6039156Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6039563Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6039826Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6040366Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6041009Z     shift
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6041503Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6042050Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6042670Z     else
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6043189Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6043809Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6044586Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6045133Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6045667Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6046182Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6046525Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6046932Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6047729Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6048493Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6049329Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6049877Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6050294Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6050650Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6051080Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6051558Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6052569Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6053351Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6054345Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6055205Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6069018Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6070326Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6071226Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6071738Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6072081Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6072921Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6073825Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6074742Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6075070Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6075675Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6076226Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6076791Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6077320Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6077852Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6078475Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6079041Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6079596Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6080113Z         ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6080572Z esac
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6080890Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6081192Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6082001Z success() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6082531Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6083345Z     return 0
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6083805Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6084369Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6084702Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6085288Z failure() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6085791Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6086364Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6087119Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6087785Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6088247Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6088554Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6088824Z echo_success() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6089329Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6089935Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-24T00:29:17.6100016Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6100288Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6100747Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6101260Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6101742Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6102276Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6102870Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6103354Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6103828Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6104718Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6105360Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6105874Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6106381Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6106925Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6107533Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6107987Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6108531Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6109272Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6109938Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6110402Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6110887Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6111497Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6112074Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6112523Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6113032Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6113646Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6114657Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6115159Z         *)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6115735Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6116211Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6116571Z     esac
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6117095Z }

2026-10-24T00:29:17.6180885Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6181608Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6182189Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6182854Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6183517Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6184347Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6184680Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6185086Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6185823Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6186639Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6187615Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6188185Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6188882Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6189801Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6190608Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6191102Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6196349Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6197721Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6198505Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6198858Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6199627Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6200339Z     echo
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6200940Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6201926Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6202920Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6203545Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6204486Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6205506Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6206352Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6206735Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6207036Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6207703Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6208560Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6209507Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6210378Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6211155Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6211898Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6212623Z }
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6213023Z
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6213417Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6214421Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6215412Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6216126Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-24T00:29:17.6217250Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
